Extracted the computation into a new Qt-free `chisurf/plugins/microscopy/sm_image_mle/core/molecule_mle.py` — `MoleculeMleSettings`, `MoleculeMleResult`, `segment_molecules` (watershed), `build_irf_vv_vh`/`compute_g_factor`, `fit_molecules(tttr, settings, clsm=…)` / `fit_molecules_from_files` — built on the shared [`fit2x` harness](/subsystems/fitting.md) (`Fit2x`/`assemble_vv_vh`), returning a per-molecule DataFrame + label/intensity images + centroids (no more matplotlib PNG dumps or TSV writes in the compute layer). `api/molecule_mle.py::analyze_request` now runs the core **in-process** (loop files → per-file `molecule_data.tsv` + merged `joint_output.tsv`), mirroring the pixel-wise sibling. Replaced the whole old GUI with an **AutoForm view.json** (`gui/molecule_mle.view.json`) over a Qt-free `MoleculeMleViewModel` (`path_list` file/IRF inputs, value/toggle settings, a `▶ Run` `button_row` launched on a `QThreadPool` worker, an `image` dock showing the segmentation with green molecule-centroid markers, and a `table` of per-molecule fits); `gui/tool.py::SmImageMleTool` is now a thin `AutoForm`-hosting `QWidget`. Deleted the monolith `sm_image_mle.py` and the dead `cli.py` shim (the `cli/` package shadowed it); fixed `cli/__init__.py` to actually export `cli`. **Verified against tttrlib-simulated CLSM images** (`SimEngine`/`SimScanner` raster of two immobile fluorophores with τ=1.0/3.5 ns → segment → fit recovers τ≈1.0/3.55 ns), and the GUI headless-screenshot-verified (image + markers + table render the simulated result). Tests (arm64): new `test/test_molecule_mle_core.py` (simulate→segment→fit, 3), `test/test_api_orchestration.py` (per-file + joint TSV, warnings, 2), rewritten `test/test_widgets.py` (view-model bindings, view-spec load, AutoForm tool, 3) — 8 pass. Manifest bumped to 2.1.0. See [plugins/imaging.md](/plugins/imaging.md) and the `fit2x` harness in [subsystems/fitting.md](/subsystems/fitting.md).
* **Calibration: reference-sample α/δ + multi-chromophore N-cube + confirmed GUI parameter-link (continuation 2).** Completed the calibration methodology and generalised it beyond two colours. (1) **Reference-sample estimators** (`chisurf/core/fluorescence/fret/calibration.py`): `leakage_from_donor_only` (α from a donor-only sample), `direct_excitation_from_acceptor_only` (δ from an acceptor-only sample), and `calibrate_from_samples` orchestrating the full Hellenkamp procedure (α from donor-only, δ from acceptor-only, γ/β from the FRET E-S fit, optional prior-regularized refine) — α/δ are now data-driven, not only the light-path prior (`test_calibration_samples.py`, recovers known α=0.08/δ=0.05/γ=1.4). (2) **Multi-chromophore (N-cube) correction** `es.py::corrected_es_matrix(intensity, gamma, alpha, delta, …)` — generalises the three-cube to an N×N `I_ij` matrix using the **coupled donor budget** `E_ij = (F_ij/γ_ij)/(F_ii + Σ_k F_ik/γ_ik)`, recovering each pairwise efficiency exactly when one donor is quenched by several acceptors (the naive pairwise formula biases `E_ij → E_ij/(1−E_ik)`); reduces exactly to `corrected_es` for two chromophores (`test_multi_chromophore.py`: N=2 reduction + 3-color exact recovery + documents the bias). (3) **GUI parameter-link auto-wired** — confirmed `ChiSurfAPI.list_fits()` serializes the registered `CalibrationFit` with its factors (the same source the parameter-link menu reads), so the shared calibration appears as a link target with no extra Qt (`test_calibration_shared.py`). 37 calibration/crosstalk tests pass, ruff clean (arm64). Only remaining: a one-click ndx toolbar push button and per-pixel/FLIM reuse (headless cores + ndx bridge already cover the substance) — see [references/fret-calibration.md](/references/fret-calibration.md). Added a **user-facing docs page** `docs/FRET calibration.md` (Sphinx toctree in `docs/index.rst`) — theory (Hellenkamp α/β/γ/δ, I_ij channels, E/S formulas), the two factor-sourcing routes (light-path prior→posterior; donor-only/acceptor-only reference samples), the multi-chromophore coupled-budget correction, global-analysis linking + ndx push, and simulation-based validation, with code examples and references (Hellenkamp 2018, Lee 2005).
* **Calibration: session-shared cross-fit linking + ndx bridge + Hellenkamp nomenclature (continuation).** Built the two cross-cutting pieces of the calibration vision on top of the calibration core (see the calibration entry below). (1) **Session-shared calibration for global analysis** — `CalibrationFit` wraps `CalibrationParameters` as a dataset-free pseudo-fit (minimal `model`+`unique_identifier` contract); `register_calibration`/`unregister_calibration` add it to `cs.fits` (+ project registry) so it appears in the parameter-link UI, and `link_to_calibration(param, calib, factor)` links any fit's correction parameter to the shared factor via chinet-port identity — refining one calibration updates every linked fit. (2) **ndxplorer bridge** — `calibration_to_ndx_constants` maps the posterior factors onto ndx's MFD constants (inverting ndx's effective `gamma=(PhiA/PhiD)/(gG/gR)`; ndx's `beta`≡direct-excitation δ), and `chisurf/plugins/ndxplorer/calibration_bridge.py::push_calibration_to_ndx` writes them into an in-process ndx window's `constants` and recomputes, so ndx's derived FRET uses the data-optimized calibration. (3) **Hellenkamp 2018 nomenclature + multi-chromophore convention** — renamed the calibration factors to α/β/γ/δ uniformly (**added the previously-missing β** excitation-flux ratio to `CalibrationParameters` and the `es.py` stoichiometry `S=(γF+F_da)/(γF+F_da+F_aa/β)`; `refine_calibration` also recovers β from the E-S fit). Channels use the general numeric convention `I_ij` = emission of chromophore j under excitation of i (donor 1, acceptor 2, second acceptor 3, …) to generalise to multi-chromophore systems, with friendly `i_dd`/`i_da`/`i_aa` (≡`I_11`/`I_12`/`I_22`) aliases kept in the user-facing API. Tests: `test/fitting/test_calibration_shared.py` (3), `test_calibration_ndx_bridge.py` (3), plus the updated `test_fret_calibration.py`/`test_calibration_simulation.py` — 27 calibration+crosstalk tests pass, ruff clean (arm64). OKF: [references/fret-calibration.md](/references/fret-calibration.md) updated (shared-fit + ndx bridge moved from "missing" to done; naming section added).
* **Pixel-wise FLIM MLE (`img_pixel_mle`): extracted a Qt-free core + real headless RPC backend.** The per-pixel maximum-likelihood computation lived inside the ~1970-line Qt wizard `imgmle.py` (`process_data`'s triple loop building `tttrlib.Fit23` + per-pixel VV/VH histograms), and the plugin's RPC `analyze` handler was a stub returning "use the GUI". Extracted the computation into a new Qt-free `chisurf/plugins/microscopy/img_pixel_mle/core/pixel_mle.py` — `PixelMleSettings`, `PixelMleResult`, `fit_pixel_lifetimes(tttr, settings)` / `fit_pixel_lifetimes_from_file` — built on the shared [`fit2x` harness](/subsystems/fitting.md) (`Fit2x`/`assemble_vv_vh`), producing the same per-pixel result table (identical column schema) plus `tau`/`rho` maps. `imgmle.py::process_data` now builds `PixelMleSettings` from the UI and drives the core with a progress callback (the interactive single-decay preview `update_fit` stays on raw `Fit23` since its plot reads tttrlib's `.data`/`.model` attrs). `backend/services.py::_handle_analyze` now runs a genuine headless path through the core (builds a prepared VV/VH IRF from the IRF file via `api.pixel_mle.prepare_irf`, fits every file, writes `<stem>_pixel_mle.csv`) instead of delegating to the wizard. Also added `convolution_stop` to `Fit2xSettings` (Fit23 accepts it; the image path passes `-1`). **Headless-verified on a real confocal FLIM image** (`tttr-data/imaging/pq/Microtime200_HH400/beads.ptu`, 50×50, ch [0,1,4,8]): 418/2500 pixels fit, median 2I*≈0.5, plausible lifetimes. Tests `chisurf/plugins/microscopy/img_pixel_mle/test/test_pixel_mle_core.py` (core fit, min-photons threshold, IRF-length guard, backend CSV round-trip) — all pass (arm64). `sm_image_mle` is already a harness-based headless CLI. Follow-up still open: GUI selector to expose `noise_model` (least-squares / 2I*) in the TCSPC decay-fit UI. See [plugins/imaging.md](/plugins/imaging.md) and the `fit2x` harness in [subsystems/fitting.md](/subsystems/fitting.md).
* **Priors on the AutoForm view-spec path (PRD-61 follow-up).** Exposed parameter priors declaratively in `.view.json`. Added an optional `priors` field (parameter-name → prior-state dict) to `ParameterGroupSection` (`chisurf/core/dataspec/__init__.py`; the loader keeps it because it is now a declared dataclass field); `AutoModelWidget._build_parameter_group` applies it via a new `_apply_section_priors` helper (validated through `chisurf.core.fitting.priors.as_prior`, invalid/unknown skipped with a warning, `None` clears) before the parameter widgets are built, so a model author can ship a default Bayesian/MAP prior with the editor. The single-parameter `fitting_parameter` custom-section factory (`chisurf/gui/autoform/sections/registry.py`) now accepts a `prior` option the same way. The interactive prior selector already reached this path (AutoForm renders `FittingParameterWidget`, whose popup carries it); this adds the *declarative* surface. Tests: 3 added to `test/gui/test_parameter_prior_widget.py` (loader round-trip, `_apply_section_priors` set/clear/ignore, end-to-end apply when the widget is built) — 10 pass; model-editor skill path green apart from the two documented pre-existing baselines (TCSPC name-resolution `IndexError`, `ParseModel` regression). Docs: [subsystems/fitting.md](/subsystems/fitting.md), [PRD-61](/prds/prd-61.md).
* **Generalized fitting with parameter priors — bounds as priors (PRD-61).** Generalized the fit objective from least squares to maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) and Bayesian sampling by letting any parameter carry a **prior**, with hard **bounds implemented as the degenerate `UniformPrior`**. New Qt-free module `chisurf/core/fitting/priors.py`: a `Prior` ABC (`lnpdf`, `residuals` for the LM/MAP contribution, `support` for hard optimiser bounds, `mode`, `get_state`, `combine`/`__mul__`) with families `UniformPrior`, `NormalPrior`, `TruncatedNormalPrior`, `HalfNormalPrior`, `LogNormalPrior`, `ExponentialPrior`, `GammaPrior`, `BetaPrior`, and `CallablePrior` — the most general form wrapping any `logpdf(x)` callback (runtime-only, not serialised); `as_prior` coerces a Prior/callable/state-dict and `prior_from_state`+`PRIOR_REGISTRY` deserialise. **MAP**: `_prior_residuals` appends each free parameter's `prior.residuals(value)` to the residual vector when `get_wres(..., include_priors=True)` (Gaussian → Tikhonov `(θ−μ)/σ`; general → signed deviance `sign(θ−mode)·√(−2·Δlnpdf)`, the same reduction as the Poisson `2I*` residuals), so the existing `leastsqbound` LM engine minimises `χ²−2·ln p(θ)` unchanged; `Fit.run`/`FitGroup.run` enable it, `get_chi2` stays data-only. **MCMC**: `lnprior` now sums per-parameter `prior.lnpdf` (uniform-box fallback). **Conjugacy**: `Prior.combine` returns closed-form conjugates (`Normal×Normal` precision-weighted, `Gamma×Gamma`, `Beta×Beta`, `Normal×Uniform → TruncatedNormal`) and a generic `ProductPrior` (summed log-densities, concatenated residuals) otherwise. **chinet**: the distribution prior's spec is stored on the parameter's `chinet.Port` (`port.prior`, in the port document/JSON) so it round-trips through pickle/JSON (`modules/chinet/chinet/port.py`); `Parameter.prior` unifies bounds and priors (live callable object in memory, serialisable spec mirrored to the port, bound surfaced as `UniformPrior`). **RPC**: new `parameter.set_prior` threaded `fitting_client` → `protocol` → `server_methods.json` → `services.parameters.set_parameter_prior` → `Parameter.prior`, plus `Fit.set_parameter_prior`. **GUI**: the per-parameter popup (`chisurf/gui/widgets/fitting/parameter_widgets.py`) replaces the raw bound-box inputs with a prior-type radio selector (**Box** default with inline lower/upper bounds, **Gaussian**, **Log-normal**, **Custom…**) and a modal `PriorEditorDialog` for advanced per-distribution parameters (all families incl. Gamma/Beta/etc). Tests (arm64): `test/fitting/test_priors.py` (41 — families, callback, conjugate combination, MAP bias/recovery, port/pickle round-trip), `test/gui/test_parameter_prior_widget.py` (7, headless, screenshot-verified), client + protocol/service contract + chinet suites green. Also fixed a latent `Parameter.get_state` bug where an unbounded port's `(None, None)` bounds raised in `float()`. Follow-ups: AutoForm/view-spec prior surface; optional named-callback registry for persistence. See [subsystems/fitting.md](/subsystems/fitting.md), [subsystems/parameters.md](/subsystems/parameters.md), and [PRD-61](/prds/prd-61.md).

## 2026-07-16
* **smFRET calibration core: light-path prior → data-optimized posterior (accurate FRET).** Calibration factors (detection/QY ratio `gamma`, donor leakage `alpha`, direct acceptor excitation `delta`, backgrounds, `R0`) are now proper `FittingParameter`s whose **prior** comes from the light-path calculator and whose **posterior** comes from optimizing against burst data — plugging into the (already-wired) priors machinery (`chisurf/core/fitting/priors.py`, `Fit.set_parameter_prior`, `_prior_residuals`/`lnprob`). New Qt-free `chisurf/core/fluorescence/fret/calibration.py`: `CalibrationParameters(FittingParameterGroup)`; `lightpath_correction_factors`/`set_priors_from_lightpath` (compute `gamma=(gR·cRA·QYA)/(gG·cGD·QYD)`, `alpha`, `delta` from a `get_crosstalk_matrices()` payload via `crosstalk.matrix_from_payload` + the `pda/nusiance.py` algebra, and attach `NormalPrior`/`TruncatedNormalPrior`); `global_es_correction` (data `gamma` from the E-S population linear fit `1/S=Ω+Σ·E`, Lee 2005 / Hellenkamp 2018) and `refine_calibration` (the **posterior**: precision-weighted Bayesian combination of the bootstrapped data estimate and the light-path prior — strong data→data, weak data→prior). New `chisurf/core/fluorescence/burst/es.py`: `apparent_es` + `corrected_es` (three-cube E and, newly, ALEX/PIE stoichiometry `S`, reusing `crosstalk.correct_three_cube`). `BurstWorkflow.simulate(gamma=…)` now bakes a known detection factor into the tttrlib photon simulation (red-brightness scaled by gamma), recorded on `GroundTruth.gamma`, so recovery is validated end-to-end. Fixed a latent bug surfaced en route (already covered) — the `nusiance` decorator's Py2 `func_globals`. Tests: `test/fitting/test_fret_calibration.py` (8: factors, priors, E/S recovery, gamma recovery, strong-vs-weak-data posterior) + `test/plugins/burst/test_calibration_simulation.py` (2, end-to-end: simulate known gamma → burst-select → BVA proximity ratio → corrected E recovers the true 0.25/0.75) — all pass (arm64). Example notebook `chisurf/plugins/burst/burst_analysis/examples/FRET_Calibration.ipynb` (runs end-to-end: light-path prior → simulate → E-S plot apparent-vs-corrected → layered calibration → tttrlib-sim recovery). Documented in the new OKF concept [references/fret-calibration.md](/references/fret-calibration.md). Deferred (documented): session-shared calibration as a dataset-free pseudo-fit in `cs.fits` for cross-fit global-analysis linking; pushing the posterior calibration into the in-process ndxplorer constants; `alpha`/`delta` from donor-only/acceptor-only samples; per-pixel/GUI. Note: found `img_pixel_rfret` would be **redundant** with ndxplorer's derived-column FRET engine — recorded in [references/crosstalk.md](/references/crosstalk.md).

## 2026-07-15
* **FCS batch 2 (6 more PAM models) + PCF experiment type + channel-aware FLCS correlator + general crosstalk core.** Continuation of the PAM-referenced FCS work, all A/B-verified against PAM at PAM's own default parameters (the "examples from PAM"; real dataset also cloned to `junk/PAM-sampledata`). (1) **6 more FCS models** in `models.yaml` (`test_fcs_pam_ab.py`, now 27 tests): `FCS_TauD_Background`, the three `FCS_SCCF_*_TauD_anomalous` (anomalous diffusion + 1/2/3 signed relaxation terms via a fixed `isCCF` sign switch for species cross-correlation dips), and `fullFCS_sum`/`fullFCS_product` (antibunching × bunching × triplet × diffusion, mixed ns/µs units). (2) **New PCF experiment type** — pair-correlation-function distribution fits, previously missing entirely. Registered in `experiment_configs.yaml` (`experiment_types.pcf` + a `pcf:` block reusing the generic CSV curve reader), with a core catalogue `chisurf/core/models/pcf/{parse.py (ParsePCFModel),models.yaml}` (`PCF_LogNormal`/`LogGaussian`/`Gamma`, the gamma model folding PAM's x-independent `1/gamma(alpha)` normaliser into the amplitude since the parser has no `gamma`) and a GUI catalogue widget `chisurf/gui/widgets/models/pcf/ParsePCFWidget`. Tests `test_pcf_pam_ab.py` (A/B incl. gamma-shape) + `test_pcf_experiment.py` (registration/reader). (3) **Channel-aware FLCS filtered correlation** — Qt-free core `chisurf/core/fluorescence/fcs/filtered.py::species_filtered_correlation`/`species_weight_streams` builds per-photon weights from a 2-D `(n_species,n_bins)` matrix, a 3-D `(n_channels,n_species,n_bins)` table, or a `{channel: matrix}` map (par/perp/per-detector, via `correlate.get_weights`), then correlates every species auto/cross pair with `tttrlib.Correlator`; `FilterResult.to_channel_filters` / `FilterResultMFD.to_channel_filters(par,perp)` produce the channel table; a plugin entrypoint `fcs_correlator/core.py::filtered_correlation_datasets/_from_tttr` emits correlator dataset dicts, and `CorrelatorSettingsModel.set_lifetime_filters` + a `correlate_data` branch wire it into the GUI model (mirrors `tttrlib.Correlator.set_filter`, which the user noted exists but whose 0.27 SWIG binding is fiddly to marshal). Tests `test_flcs_correlation.py` (5, incl. photon-level filter orthogonality + channel-aware==single-axis) and `fcs_correlator/test/test_filtered_core.py` (3). (4) **General spectral-crosstalk core** `chisurf/core/fluorescence/crosstalk.py` — the shared linear-mixing utility the user flagged as general (light-path calculator forward matrix, ratiometric/sensitized-emission FRET, phasor unmixing, DDEM): `matrix_from_payload` (build ordered numpy `M` from a `get_crosstalk_matrices()` payload), `apply_mixing`/`invert_mixing` (forward + pinv/nnls inverse, pixel-broadcasting), `correct_three_cube` (Gordon/Nagy 3-cube rFRET: `Fc = IDA − d·IDD − a·IAA`, recovers known E) and `three_cube_fret_efficiency`; plus a **bug fix** to `chisurf/core/fluorescence/intensity.py::nusiance` (Python-2 `func_globals` → `__globals__`, which had made the entire scalar FRET-correction API raise `AttributeError` at call time). Tests `test_crosstalk.py` (11, incl. mixing round-trip, 3-cube E recovery, decorator fix). Full new-feature suite: **66 pass** (arm64). Remaining (documented, not built): the `correlator.view.json` lifetime-filter file-picker UI, an `img_pixel_rfret` microscopy plugin consuming PIE green/red/yellow maps, refactoring `img_pixel_phasor.phasor_unmix` to reuse `invert_mixing`, DDEM sensitized-emission wiring onto the crosstalk core, and porting `fullFCS_pulsed_linear` (needs a coded pulse-train model). See [references/fcs-pam-port.md](/references/fcs-pam-port.md) and the new [references/crosstalk.md](/references/crosstalk.md).
* **Maximum-likelihood (2I*) fitting: shared tttrlib `fit2x` harness + first-class in-core Poisson objective.** Two coupled changes that make MLE a proper part of ChiSurf's fitting infrastructure instead of siloed plugin boilerplate. (1) New Qt-free harness `chisurf/core/fluorescence/mle/` (`fit2x.py`): `Fit2xSettings` (dt/period/IRF/background/polarisation), a reusable `Fit2x` fitter (builds the tttrlib `Fit23`/`Fit24`/`Fit25` once, fits many decays), a typed `Fit2xResult` with named accessors (`tau`/`gamma`/`r_scatter`/`r_experimental`, `as_dict`), and `assemble_vv_vh` for the parallel|perpendicular layout. It is the single seam around tttrlib's Maus-2001 `2I*` MLE — the estimator itself is already good C++; only ChiSurf's harness around it was poor (the raw `tttrlib.Fit23(dt=…, p2s_twoIstar_flag=…)` construction + `res['x']`/`res['twoIstar']` parsing was copy-pasted across four call sites). Migrated the burst-MLE batch worker (`burst_mle_analysis/_mp_worker.py`), the wizard's batch pass, and `microscopy/sm_image_mle` onto the harness; the wizard interactive-preview and the `img_pixel_mle` monolith still call raw `Fit23` and are the next consumers to move. (2) Added a selectable `noise_model` on `Fit`: `"default"` keeps weighted-least-squares (Neyman) exactly as before; `"poisson"` (aliases `mle`/`2istar`) returns the **signed Poisson deviance residuals** `sign(μ−y)·√(2·(μ−y+y·ln(y/μ)))` from the new `deviance_residuals` helper, so `Σr²=2I*` and the *existing* `leastsqbound` LM engine, `chi2`/`chi2r`, covariance, F-test and residual plots all minimise the Poisson likelihood unchanged (Laurence & Chromy 2010) — the correct estimator for low-count TCSPC/FLIM/burst decays. Threaded `Fit.noise_model → Model.get_wres → calculate_weighted_residuals`; PDA models and every default fit are byte-unchanged. Tests: `test/fluorescence/test_mle_fit2x.py` (harness: VV/VH assembly, settings validation, `Fit23` lifetime recovery, fitter reuse) and `test/fitting/test_poisson_mle.py` (deviance≡2I* identity, zero/perfect-bin handling, selector plumbing, WLS back-compat, Poisson fit recovers params with reduced 2I*≈1) — 12 pass (arm64). No regressions (the 4 pre-existing `test_fit.py` standalone-invocation failures fail identically on baseline). Docs updated: [subsystems/fitting.md](/subsystems/fitting.md), [plugins/burst.md](/plugins/burst.md). This is phase 1 of a larger effort (H2MM/FRETBursts benchmarks in docs; FRET-2CDE + DCBS-in-tttrlib feeding ndxplorer) — see the `mle-fretbursts-benchmark-resume` memory.
* **FCS model catalogue expansion (PAM port, A/B-verified) + FLCS filter fixes.** Ported PAM's (Schrimpf 2018) FCS fit-model catalogue into `chisurf/core/models/fcs/models.yaml` as 13 absolute-`D`, physically-parametrised models (x in s; D µm²/s; w µm; tau µs): `FCS_D`, `FCS_D_2Component`, `FCS_D_2exp`, `FCS_TauD`, `FCS_TauD_anomalous`, `FCS_flow`, `FCS_D_Background`, `FCS_D_afterpulsing`, `FCS_TauD_Bleaching`, `FCS_2Focus_D` (two-focus absolute `D`), `FCS_FRETFCCS` (2-state kinetics), `nsFCS_ab_cd_triplet`, `FCS_Scanning`. Each carries the γ=1/√8 prefactor and triplet term. **A/B-verified**: `test/fitting/test_fcs_pam_ab.py` transcribes each PAM MATLAB `fit` lambda verbatim (keeping the `1e-12`/`1e-6` SI factors, which cancel in the diffusion terms → reduced `4·D·x/w²`) and asserts equality to the ChiSurf equation over random parameters (evaluated through the real `ParseModel` scanner). This replaced the initial hand-rolled `w0/s` two-focus entries. A catalogue-wide parse test also caught and fixed **3 pre-existing broken entries** (`3D Gauss, 1 exp. bunch, 1 stretched exp. bunch` — unbalanced parens + missing `a1`/`td1`; `Autocorrelation with biexponential anisotropy term` — `td`→`td1`; `1x2D Gauss, 1x3D Gauss, 1 bunching + 5 anti-correlation` — missing `s`), whose equations referenced parameters absent from their `initial:` blocks. **FLCS filters** (`chisurf/core/fluorescence/fcs/filtered.py`, PAM `Calc_fFCS_Filters` as reference): the weighted-pseudo-inverse algebra was already correct; added `rcond`/`tikhonov` conditioning + `filter_condition_number` diagnostic (for near-collinear/similar-lifetime patterns), `uniform_pattern` (flat afterpulsing/dark-count species → afterpulse-free FLCS), and `photon_filter_weights` (per-photon micro-time→filter weight, the core for wiring filters into a weighted correlator); fixed the legacy `calc_lifetime_filter` divide-by-zero (zero-bin guard) and removed its nonstandard global renormalisation that broke the `Σ_t R_k(t)·p_j(t)=δ_kj` relation. Tests: `test/fitting/test_fcs_pam_ab.py`, `test_fcs_2ffcs.py`, `test_fcs_filters.py` — 31 pass (arm64 env). Remaining gaps (correlator wiring of filters, channel-aware par/perp weighting, scatter/IRF & model-generated pattern species, non-Gaussian Dertinger MDF, and the pCF/SCCF/MIA models) documented in the new OKF concept [references/fcs-pam-port.md](/references/fcs-pam-port.md); [references/two-focus-fcs.md](/references/two-focus-fcs.md) and [plugins/fcs.md](/plugins/fcs.md) updated. PAM cloned under `junk/PAM` as reference.
* **Two-focus FCS (2fFCS) model functions + status doc.** Added two absolute-diffusion model functions to `chisurf/core/models/fcs/models.yaml` — `2f-FCS auto-correlation (absolute D)` and `2f-FCS cross-correlation (absolute D)` — written in the `D`/`w0` parametrisation (Dertinger 2007). The cross-correlation carries the known inter-focus distance `d` in an `exp(-d²/(w0²+4Dx))` term, so a global fit sharing `D`/`w0`/`s` (with `d` fixed) yields an **absolute** diffusion coefficient without a separately calibrated volume; it correctly peaks at a finite lag and reduces exactly to the auto-correlation at `d=0`. Verified through the real `ParseModel` scanner (params resolve to `N,D,w0,s,d,b`); headless test `test/fitting/test_fcs_2ffcs.py` (5 tests) asserts the physics. Documented what's implemented vs missing in a new OKF concept [references/two-focus-fcs.md](/references/two-focus-fcs.md) (missing: Dertinger's non-Gaussian modified detection function, which needs `erf` — unavailable in the string-equation evaluator's NumPy-only scope, so it needs an `erf` binding or a coded FCS model class; plus focus-distance calibration, a dedicated 2f channel-pairing preset, a guided global-fit GUI, triplet/multi-component variants, and an end-to-end absolute-`D` recovery test). Linked from [plugins/fcs.md](/plugins/fcs.md) and the references index.
* **New plugin `burst_ebfret` — binned-trajectory smFRET HMM (ebFRET port).** Closes the one confocal/TIRF gap the burst group lacked: analysis of **binned intensity-vs-time FRET traces** (wide-field/camera data) rather than photon streams. Added `chisurf/plugins/burst/burst_ebfret/` — a Qt-free, NumPy/SciPy-only port of ebFRET (van de Meent et al.; a re-implementation of vbFRET): `core/vbem.py` (per-trace variational-Bayes EM over a univariate Gaussian-emission HMM — Dirichlet priors on initial/transition, Normal-Gamma emission prior in ebFRET's `(m,β,a,b)` form, linear-scaled forward-backward), `core/ebayes.py` (empirical-Bayes outer loop with conjugate h-step prior updates ported from ebFRET's `+dist/{dirichlet,normgamma}/h_step.m` — Dirichlet Newton + Normal-Gamma moment-matching), `core/viterbi.py`, `core/analysis.py` (K-scan + evidence selection + dwell/transition decoding), `io.py` (ebFRET stacked `.dat` loader), `api/models.py`, `backend/services.py` (`burst_ebfret.jobs.compute` RPC), `cli/main.py` (`ebfret compute`). **Validated against ebFRET's own `simulated-K04-N350` fixture** (vendored under `tests/data/`, 350 traces / 39,595 frames): at K=4 the port recovers `[0.03, 0.34, 0.51, 0.62]`, the two well-separated interior states within 0.05 of ebFRET's fitted `[0.33, 0.516]` — endpoints differ only because this port fits the uncorrected proximity ratio `A/(D+A)` whereas ebFRET fits a background-subtracted signal (donor-only near 0). Tests: 7 fast synthetic unit tests + 4 recovery/integration tests (3 `slow`) all pass (arm64 env; pixi env solve is currently broken on an unrelated `msgpack` candidate). Porting this one algorithm covers the functionality of four MATLAB-only TIRF tools on fret.community (**ebFRET, vbFRET, HaMMy, SMACKS**). Scope: single-prior D=1 path; prior-mixture subpopulations, SMD/session I/O, and a GUI tool are follow-ups. Marked `experimental`/`menu_hidden`. Grew out of a fret.community/software gap analysis vs ChiSurf (repos cloned into `junk/`). Other gaps assessed (not built): diffusion-FRET photon simulation is already substantially in **tttrlib** (`~/dev/tttrlib`, PRD-005 Burbulator port — 3D Brownian + PSF + FRET + TCSPC + CLSM, SPC-132 byte-exact; remaining: periodic/mirror BCs, Photon-HDF5 write, single-molecule throughput) and needs only surfacing via chisurf **PRD-53**; rFRET ratiometric + FLIM crosstalk correction can reuse the existing forward crosstalk-matrix builder (`plugins/core/lightpath_simulator/backend/simulator.py::get_crosstalk_matrices`), which is currently never inverted to correct measured data; wide-field camera-movie TIRF (spot localization/trace extraction) remains a genuine large gap but is pure-intensity and off ChiSurf's photon-centric path (only PAM's TIFF-stack phasor-FLIM ingest is FLIM-relevant). See [plugins/burst.md](/plugins/burst.md).
* **Extracted MMFDB into a self-contained sibling repository.** `modules/mmfdb` (178 in-tree files) was moved out to `~/dev/mmfdb` as a standalone, independently versioned package destined for `github.com/Fluorescence-Tools/mmfdb`, and chisurf now consumes it via a **symlink** `modules/mmfdb -> ../../mmfdb` (the established `imp-tricks` pattern). This leaned on chisurf being pre-wired for the split: the `sys.path` bootstrap (`chisurf/_bundled_packages.py`) resolves `modules/mmfdb/src` through the symlink unchanged, `pyproject.toml` already declares `mmfdb==0.1.0` and excludes it from `packages.find`, and `test/core/test_mmfdb_vendor_package.py` still passes (8/8). **New repo** (`~/dev/mmfdb`, `git init`, 202 files, 631 tests pass, docs build `-W` clean): dropped `EXTRACTION.md` + the stray `examples/None`; added own `.gitignore`, `MANIFEST.in`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, an `examples` extra (`tttrlib`/`fretbursts`/`phconvert`) and moved `test_min_client.py` into `tests/`; reframed `README.md` and fixed all `modules/mmfdb/…` doc paths to package-relative; host-neutrality still guarded by `tests/test_architecture.py`. **chisurf side:** removed `test/plugins/mmfdb/` (moved) and the redundant root `docker-compose.mmfdb.yml`; added an mmfdb sibling-clone/install step to `rattler-recipe/{build.sh,build.bat}` and the three CI workflows (closes the prior gap where the packaged app installed every local module *except* mmfdb); synced the `mmfdb_admin` manifest (added the pre-existing-undeclared `fluorophores.import_default_set`). Verified end-to-end through the symlink: vendor boundary, burst-workflow facade, H2MM-simulation, and notebook discovery all pass. **Examples decision:** the 8 chisurf-based notebooks stay in mmfdb with chisurf as an optional extra; docs render them non-executed (`nb_execution_mode="off"`), so the mmfdb doc build needs no chisurf. Nothing pushed — creating/pushing `Fluorescence-Tools/mmfdb` and the coordinated chisurf commit are left to the user.
* **MMFDB slop cleanup.** Removed AI-generated cruft from `modules/mmfdb`: deleted a stray 950 KB SQLite DB accidentally written to `examples/None`; trimmed docstring puffery ("comprehensive parser" in `schema/pdbx_metadata.py`, "first-class read service" in `provenance/lineage.py`); and deleted six provably-dead legacy getters on `SampleDefinition` in `src/mmfdb/models.py` that had zero callers repo-wide.
* **MMFDB: drop the dual-field legacy sample model (pre-release, no back-compat needed).** Removed the entire flat/structured legacy layer that duplicated the flrCIF/PRD-02 model. `ProbeDefinition` lost `position`/`position_label`/`chain_id`/`residue_name` (and their `__post_init__` mapping to `seq_id`/`comp_id`/`asym_id`/`auth_name`); callers now pass flrCIF fields directly. `SampleDefinition` lost `entity_name`/`entity_sequence`/`entity_type`, the flat `donor_probe_name`/`donor_position`/`donor_position_label` (+ acceptor equivalents), and the deprecated structured `donor`/`acceptor` `ProbeDefinition` fields — everything now goes through `entities`/`probes`/`fret_pairs`. `_validate_vocabulary` validates each entity's `entity_type` (still hard-rejects unknowns with the same "Invalid entity_type" message) instead of a flat field. Migrated consumers: `samples/sample_manager.py` (`_metadata_from_definition`, `_collect_all_probes`, `_insert_entity`, `_derive_fluorophore_types`, `_get_probe_description`, `_sample_type`/key-values now read the first entity's type; probe `description` sources `auth_name` not `position_label`), `samples/sample_requests.py` (`SampleCreateRequest`/`SampleUpdateRequest` de-legacied), `admin/backend/services.py` `_normalize_probe_payload` (still accepts `residue_number`/`residue_name`/`chain_id`/`position` input aliases, emits only flrCIF keys), and `chisurf/gui/widgets/sample_picker.py` (builds `probes=[ProbeDefinition(...)]` + a `FretPairDefinition` from the donor/acceptor dye fields instead of the flat kwargs — dyes are now recorded as real sample probes with correct donor/acceptor roles). Updated `test/fio/test_sample_manager.py` and `modules/mmfdb/tests/test_prd19_collapse.py` to the new API. Verified: mmfdb standalone suite **628 passed / 2 skipped**, `test/fio/test_sample_manager.py` **21 passed**, mmfdb_admin plugin tests green, plus an end-to-end round-trip (FRET sample → DB → correct donor/acceptor roles, `residue_number`/`asym_id`/`residue_name` positions, conditions). Three `test/fio/test_dataset_browser.py` failures are pre-existing (auth/object-store env, fail on baseline without these changes). See [architecture/mmfdb.md](/architecture/mmfdb.md).

## 2026-07-13
* **General non-programmer facade for the MMFDB burst workflow.** Added `chisurf/plugins/burst/burst_analysis/api/workflow.py` — a Qt-free `BurstWorkflow` facade that collapses the whole remote pipeline (start/connect MMFDB server + login → register dataset(s) → fetch-by-handle → burst selection → BVA → H2MM) into one plain-English line per step, so a bench scientist needs no settings dataclasses, object UUIDs, or TTTR bookkeeping. `BurstWorkflow.demo()` spins up a private in-process server for tutorials; `.connect(url, user, password)` targets a real deployment. Generality: a declarable `Setup` of named `Detector`s (routing channels + optional micro-time windows), built via `Setup.from_channels(green=…, red=…, yellow=…)` for any number of dyes and mirroring ChiSurf's `{name:{chs,micro_time_ranges}}` model. FRET is not baked into the setup (no donor/acceptor or "two-colour" naming) — the pair is chosen per analysis (`bva("green","red")`, `h2mm(donor=…, acceptor=…)`), defaulting to the first two detectors, while H2MM models every detector as a stream (true N-colour). Optional/inferred `file_type`; `register_all` + multi-dataset `select_bursts`; and a selectable search `method` (default `"burst"` sliding-window, which — unlike the old `filter_active=False` grouping — actually honours `min_photons`). `Bursts.bva()` / `.h2mm(states=…)` return `Bva`/`H2mm` results carrying `.plot()`, `.summary()`, `mean_proximity_ratio`, `dynamic_fraction`. Rewrote `modules/mmfdb/examples/mmfdb_00_overview.ipynb` (executes end-to-end: 3 datasets, 488 bursts, BVA 41 % dynamic fraction, H2MM 3-state fit) and `test/plugins/burst/test_mmfdb_burst_workflow.py` (8 tests against a live demo server: handle round-trip, multi-dataset selection, explicit-vs-default setup, three-colour setup with a chosen FRET pair, micro-time-gated detectors, setup validation, BVA ranges, deterministic 2-state H2MM).

The example notebooks are a proper **myst-nb gallery**: `conf.py` uses `myst_nb` (`nb_execution_mode = "off"` — the notebooks already carry test-run outputs), a `docs/examples` symlink exposes them to the source tree, and `index.md` lists them with a `{toctree}` glob (`examples/mmfdb_*`) instead of hand-coded links — each renders as a full page with its stored plots, and `usage`/`concepts` link them with `{doc}`. **Verified `sphinx-build -W` builds all 17 pages (6 docs + 9 rendered notebooks) with zero warnings.** `rpc-clients.md` is a **tight wire-contract spec** (JSON-RPC 2.0 over `/rpc`, bearer-token auth, `/objects` upload/download, method namespaces) so an agent can implement a client in any language without reading chisurf; it `literalinclude`s a ~50-line stdlib-only reference client `modules/mmfdb/examples/external_tools/mmfdb_min_client.py`, kept honest by `test/plugins/mmfdb/test_min_client.py` (login + object round-trip + JSON-RPC call/error against a live chisurf-free server). * **Rebrand MFDB → MMFDB (multimodal fluorescence database).** Full identifier rename across the tree: the Python package `modules/mfdb/src/mfdb` → `modules/mmfdb/src/mmfdb` (dist name `mmfdb`, `mmfdb-admin` console script, all `from mmfdb.*` imports), the `mmfdb_admin` plugin, `chisurf/gui/widgets/mmfdb`, `chisurf/core/mmfdb_services.py`, `chisurf/core/project/mmfdb_adapter.py`, the plugin `api/mmfdb.py` modules (burst/tttr/ndxplorer/spectra_downloader/_dev), the `mmfdb_flr_ext.dic` dictionary, tests, docs, and OKF concepts (`architecture/mmfdb.md`, `specs/mmfdb.md`, `references/mmfdb-*`, `plugins/profiles/mmfdb-admin.md`). **Data schema renamed too:** mmCIF categories `_mfdb_*` → `_mmfdb_*`, the `category_prefix`/`_MMFDB_TABLE_PREFIXES`, and every SQL table `mfdb_*` → `mmfdb_*`. Added schema **v42** migration (`SCHEMA_VERSION` 41 → 42): `_rename_legacy_mfdb_tables` runs up front in `migrate_schema` — before any reconcile migration — renaming existing `mfdb_*` tables in place (idempotent; drops only empty stale targets), so live databases keep their data; the `_schema_version` fallback keeps old DBs reporting v41 so the migration fires. Verified: synthetic v41 DB + shipped `spectra.db` (v40) migrate with data preserved and no leftover `mfdb_*` tables; standalone mmfdb suite **499 passed**, `mmfdb_admin` **111 passed**, fluorophore_db **33 passed**, chisurf core/fio/plugin mmfdb tests green. (Note: `grep -I` silently skipped several UTF-8 files as "binary" during the bulk pass — CLAUDE.md, which is gitignored, and the `_dev/fluorophore_db` files — caught and fixed via a per-file scan.)

## 2026-07-11
* **MMFDB prerelease architecture hard cut.** Made `modules/mmfdb` a real host-neutral package and reduced the surface before release: `import mmfdb` now exposes only `MFDatabase` plus runtime configuration (lazy repository import instead of 123 eager exports); all ChiSurf dependencies were removed from `src/mmfdb` and locked with an AST guard; all ChiSurf callers were cut over to direct `mmfdb.*` imports and the `chisurf.core.mmfdb` facade/meta-path aliasing was deleted. ChiSurf-specific fit serialization/parameter-name lookup, Burst execution, deterministic fluorophore triage, and pipeline browsing now enter through explicit callable injection in `chisurf/core/project/mmfdb_adapter.py` and `chisurf/core/mmfdb_services.py`. Added one request-scoped `APIRequestContext` so `mmfdb.v1.*` opens/authenticates exactly one database and no longer mutates dispatcher params. Schema repair is now explicit v41 migration work: fresh databases build the current declarative baseline directly, existing v40 databases reconcile once, current opens have no bootstrap/reconcile side effects, and `readonly=True` is enforced by SQLite. Removed shadowed duplicate methods, dead materialized-view code, private repository utility re-exports, and prerelease setup-definition aliases; corrected the GUI client's duplicated lifecycle/reagent namespaces and aligned the manifest with registered auth methods. Updated the package/extraction architecture, completed PRD-24, and refreshed the cleanup assessment. Verification from the finished tree: standalone **481 passed**; ChiSurf `test/fio` **237 passed, 12 skipped**; MMFDB Admin **111 passed**; architecture/schema **15 passed**; additional MMFDB integrations **21 passed**; Ruff Pyflakes/import checks + compileall + `git diff --check` pass; standalone wheel builds successfully.

## 2026-07-10
* **Burst — H2MM experimental flag now actually shows in the workflow.** The `manifest.json` `experimental: true` flag only drives the nav banner when H2MM is launched as its *own* plugin; embedded as **step 6 of the `burst_analysis` shell** (how it's normally used), the `NavigationPanelTool` reads the *panel dict*, which lacked the flag — so no ⚠/banner appeared. Added `experimental: true` + `experimental_message` to the H2MM entry in `burst_analysis`'s `BURST_PANELS`, so the step label renders `6. H2MM  ⚠` and a warning banner is prepended to the panel. Reverted the earlier status-label hack (it got overwritten by "Data folder: …"); kept the standalone-window title suffix. New guard test `test_h2mm_panel_is_flagged_experimental`; burst_analysis + GUI tests green.
* **Burst — H2MM re-affirmed experimental.** Kept the `manifest.json` `experimental: true` flag (drives the NavigationPanelTool banner + nav ⚠) and refreshed its stale `experimental_message` (it claimed "not yet cross-validated against H2MM_C" — now A/B-validated on *simulated* data, but still not on measured experimental smFRET, and the GUI defaults to the approximate float32 engine). Added a visible in-window marker (title suffix `⚠ experimental` + an amber status note) so the standalone tool signals its status even when opened outside the navigation panel. Treat results as preliminary.
* **Burst — H2MM GUI: per-iteration progress so long single fits don't look stuck.** A real SPC-132 dataset (`bh_spc132_sm_dna/…burstwise_All 0.1500#60`) appeared to "hang on 1/3 state counts". Diagnosed: not hung — with unscaled ~µs macro times the data has **139,013 unique inter-photon Δt** (`n_slots`), so each EM map rebuilds that many `A^Δt`/ρ propagators → **171 ms/map → ~85 s for one k=2 fit** (k=1 is trivial, so the bar sat at 1/3 while k=2 ground). Fix: threaded a per-EM-map `on_iter(done, max_iter)` callback through `optimize`→`_squarem`/`_plain_em`→`fit_states`(restart-aware)→`fit_one`→`scan_states`, so the scan reports **fractional** progress (completed fits + fraction of the current fit). The GUI now advances the bar smoothly with a formatted **ETA** (s/min/h), throttles per-iteration ticks to ~10 Hz, and refreshes the live plots only on fit completion. Also added a log hint recommending a higher `time_scale` when `n_slots` is huge (`time_scale=100` → 3042 slots, ~46× faster; physically appropriate for ms-scale FRET vs 50 ns macro resolution). Lint clean; GUI + engine tests green.
* **Burst — H2MM GUI: non-blocking fit + ETA progress + live plots; numba CPU scale-up + fastest default.** Made the H2MM GUI responsive and fast. The fit now runs **off the UI thread** (`chisurf.gui.widgets.progress.Worker` on the global `QThreadPool`) behind an `EnhancedProgressDialog` showing a computed **ETA** and a Cancel button (cancellation via a `threading.Event` checked in the progress callback → `_FitCancelled`). The model-selection (BIC/ICL) and FRET-state plots **update live** after each state-count fit, driven by a new `progress(done, total, fits)` callback threaded through `analyze`→`scan_states`→`run_analysis`. The GUI now defaults to the **fastest method** (`em-float32` engine + `patience=1` early-stop). Critically, **scaled up numba threads**: `env_bootstrap`/shipped `settings_chisurf.yaml` pinned `numba_num_threads: "1"`, so the whole parallel engine ran single-threaded in-app — changed the shipped default to `"auto"` (resolved to CPU-cores − 1; MKL/OMP stay 1 to avoid oversubscription) and flipped the local `~/.chisurf` value; verified it now resolves to 7 on this 8-core machine. Removed the old modal `_ProgressDialog`. New GUI tests (fastest-default, live-scan-plot helper, non-blocking worker end-to-end via a monkeypatched `run_analysis` that still drives progress); **all burst_h2mm tests green**; lint clean.
* **Burst — H2MM plugin polish: engine select, defined ndX output, CLI/GUI, notebook, docs.** Rounded the H2MM plugin into a finished tool. **Engine selection** end-to-end: `H2mmSettings` gains `engine`/`refine_iters`/`patience`/`surrogate_path`/`write_photons`; the GUI settings tab has an engine dropdown + scan-patience spinner, the `h2mm compute` CLI has `--engine`/`--surrogate`/`--patience`/`--refine-iters`/`--no-photons`, and both flow through `analyze`. **Defined output openable in ndxplorer**: `core/export.py` builds a **per-photon** table (Viterbi `State` + macro/micro time, channel, stream) and a **per-burst** summary, written as `<folder>/h2mm/h2mm_photons.h5` (pandas key `results`) + `h2mm_bursts.csv` — plain numeric tables with a `Mean Macro Time (s)` axis that ndX opens directly; `photons.py` extended with `PhotonMeta` + `return_meta` so per-photon macro/micro/channel align with the engine layout. **Notebook** `examples/notebooks/H2MM_01_Simulated_smFRET.ipynb` (linear, matplotlib, no `__main__`): simulate smFRET via `tttrlib` → extract → fit/select → Viterbi trajectory → engine comparison → ndX export; validated end-to-end via `nbconvert --execute`. **Docs** `docs/H2MM.md` (theory: photon-by-photon HMM, the `A^Δt` variable-Δt trick, Baum-Welch, BIC/ICL, Viterbi; engines; outputs; CLI/API) registered in `docs/index.rst`. New `tests/test_export.py` (meta alignment, table schema/numeric-only, HDF5/CSV round-trip). **30 burst_h2mm tests green** (incl. GUI instantiation with the new dropdown); lint clean.
* **Burst — H2MM model-selection early-stopping + SIMD/scan-trick dead-ends recorded.** Explored further *algorithmic* speed-ups now that per-fit exact EM is at the roofline. (1) **Early-stop the state-count scan** (`scan_states`/`analyze` opt-in `patience`): the BIC/ICL curve is U-shaped and the over-fit high-`k` fits are the *most expensive* — a redundant state makes a flat likelihood ridge so they run to `max_iter` without converging (measured: k=1..5 on 2-state data took 5/24/500/500/500 maps). Stopping once the criterion has risen for `patience+1` counts gave a **measured 1.65× faster scan** with identical selection; `patience=None` (default) is unchanged. (2) **SIMD confirmed a dead end by experiment:** reordering the forward matmul from a `k`-reduction to a supposedly-SIMD-friendly `i`-inner AXPY made it **0.3–0.7× (slower)** at n=2..5 — LLVM already register-optimises the small unrolled reduction, and over-burst SoA batching is structurally blocked by H2MM's variable-Δt design (each burst/photon needs a *different* transition matrix → per-lane gather, no uniform SIMD op). Not adopted. (3) Skipping per-`k` Viterbi for BIC selection is negligible (<1 % of a fit) — not adopted. New early-stop test; lint clean. Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **Burst — H2MM memory-bandwidth optimisations + unified engine selector.** The E-step is memory-bound, so two cache/bandwidth tricks (both numerically identical — A/B-vs-`H2MM_C` still exact): (1) **cache locality** — `α`/`scale` moved from a shared `(N, n)` array to small **per-thread scratch buffers** (sized to the longest burst, reused across bursts, hot in L1/L2), eliminating the largest DRAM stream. A **controlled isolated A/B** at 2.4M photons (one EM map) measures **1.25–1.6× on the E-step** (biggest at `n=3`), plus a *flat* ~10 ns/photon from 240k→2.4M (the old array caused a large-`N` cliff) and tens-of-MB lower peak memory. (2) **narrow integers** — `streams`→`int8`, `gap_slot`→`int16` (from `int32`): shrinks the index-array footprint ~3× but, once `α` is out of DRAM, is **speed-neutral** (isolated A/B 0.94–1.11×, within noise). **Correction:** an earlier note here claimed ~38 % from the int shrink at `n=2` — that was measurement noise on a loaded machine; the controlled A/B shows the cache-locality buffers are the real win and the int shrink is kept for footprint, not throughput. Confirmed SIMD is not worth a rewrite (state dim n=2–5 too small; over-burst SoA vectorisation eaten by memory bandwidth) and float32 already captures the easy width/bandwidth ~2×. Added a single **compute-engine selector** `core/engines.py` (`em`/`em-float32`/`surrogate`/`surrogate-refine`) threaded through `fit_states`(new `single_precision`)/`scan_states`/`analyze`; surrogate engines fall back to exact EM when no trained model is supplied, and model selection always scores by BIC/ICL. 19 burst_h2mm tests green; lint clean. (CLI/GUI engine wiring, ndX-openable per-photon output, notebook + docs still to land.) Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **Burst — optional amortised (surrogate) neural estimator for H2MM (PRD-60, draft).** After proving the remaining H2MM speed-up can only come from cutting EM's iteration count — and that *initialisation can't do it* (seeding Baum-Welch from the exact generative parameters still took ~39 maps vs ~42 random; only starting from the dataset's own MLE hit the ~6-map floor, because EM's cost is the finite-sample last mile) — added a **simulation-based-inference** fast path that *replaces* EM: `core/surrogate.py` trains a small scikit-learn MLP once on simulator draws to predict `(prior, trans, obs)` in one forward pass. POC: on held-out 2-state data the surrogate recovered per-state FRET to **0.028–0.032** mean-abs-error vs **0.047–0.052** for a single EM fit (more accurate — it's a Bayes estimator under the training prior — and faster, the gap widening with N). Module is Qt-free, `numpy`+`scikit-learn` only (already a dep), permutation-invariant features (windowed-FRET histogram/quantiles + photon-lag autocorrelation + Δt stats, versioned), canonical-ordered/`log10`-transition encoding, fast O(photons) training simulator, `save`/`load`, and an optional `refine_iters` EM polish. Wired as opt-in `fit_states(surrogate=…, refine_iters=…)` (default `None` → EM unchanged); ships **disabled, no pretrained model**, EM fallback when scikit-learn absent. Approximate — for over-determined data/exploration, not final numbers or model selection. Also concluded (measured) that GPU offload is a dead end here: on the M1 Pro, jax-metal couldn't compile the forward-backward `lax.scan`, and a single Metal batched-matmul step (0.786 ms) already ≈ numba's entire 240k-photon forward — the tiny-state sequential recurrence can't use a GPU. 5 surrogate tests; **26 burst_h2mm tests green**. New concept [PRD-60](/prds/prd-60.md); documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **MMFDB — metadata→edge materialization + resolvable audit labels (ELN crosslinking lessons).** Studied a mature ELN's information-management/crosslinking model and adopted two provenance-integrity wins. (1) A sample/experiment key-value whose value is an `mmfdb://<node_type>/<node_id>` reference now **materializes an idempotent `mmfdb_edge`** (`linked_to`, tagged with the originating metadata key) from the owning node to the referenced node — the ELN pattern "a metadata field that references another entity *is* a link" (`queries/artifacts.py::parse_node_ref`/`_materialize_metadata_ref`, wired into `set_sample_key_value`/`set_experiment_key_value`). (2) Edge/link creation audit entries (`add_edge`, `record_operation_link`) now carry a **resolvable target label** (the node's human title via `_resolve_node_label`), mirroring the ELN's changelog anchors. New `tests/test_metadata_edges.py` (5). Confirmed MMFDB's single polymorphic `mmfdb_edge` is architecturally ahead of the ELN's ~25-table per-pair junction matrix (do not copy it), and its `.dic`-driven vocab beats freeform extra_fields for a databank. Deferred as an **idea for later**: an optional polymorphic **tags** layer (scoped, AND-searchable) — recorded in [ELN crosslinking lessons](/references/eln-crosslinking-lessons.md). Standalone **471 passed, 1 skipped**; `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing).

## 2026-07-08
* **Burst — H2MM Viterbi parallelised over bursts; state-splitting evaluated and rejected.** (a) `viterbi()` still ran a serial Python loop over bursts while the E-step was parallel; added `_viterbi_all` (a `prange` mirror — each burst decodes a disjoint `path[s:e]` with its own work arrays, path log-liks reduce), **≈3.2× faster** (6.8→2.1 ms on 300k photons, identical paths) and on the hot path of every model-selection scan (ICL) and final decode. (b) Tried warm-starting the multi-state scan by **state-splitting** the converged `(k-1)`-state fit (SMEM-style): empirically **unsafe** — a single split cannot undo the state merging in the smaller fit, so on well-separated 3-state data it reached a *worse* k=3 optimum (−251346 vs random −236009) yet a large gain-over-parent made naive "trust" heuristics accept it; the only safe variant (augment + always take best) is *slower*, not faster. Reverted the scan changes and removed the `split_model` helper; documented the negative result in `scan_states` and [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md). A robust version needs full split+merge SMEM (out of scope). **21 burst_h2mm tests green.**
* **MMFDB — collapsed the auth provider registry to fixed local+ldap dispatch (PRD-59).** No auth providers beyond local + LDAP are planned, so the open extension mechanism was removed: deleted `register_provider`/`build_provider`/`available_providers`/`ProviderContext`/`_PROVIDER_FACTORIES` from `security/auth_providers.py`, and `login.resolve_provider` is again a fixed `local`/`ldap` dispatch (unknown → `AuthError`); the local-always-available fallback now constructs `LocalAuthProvider` directly. The `AuthProvider` protocol + `LocalAuthProvider`/`LdapAuthProvider` remain (the protocol is the seam if a provider is ever needed). Swapped the three registry tests in `test_auth_hardening.py` for a `resolve_provider` dispatch test; updated PRD-59 (removed OIDC/SAML + external-IdP as non-goals → "no further providers planned"), `architecture/mmfdb.md`, and HANDOVER. Standalone **466 passed, 1 skipped**; auth suites green. Advances [PRD-59](/prds/prd-59.md).
* **Burst — H2MM engine: four compounding speedups (E-step fusion, SQUAREM, spectral build, float32).** Second optimisation pass on `core/h2mm.py`, each gated by the A/B-vs-`H2MM_C` tests. (1) **Two-pass E-step:** folded the γ (occupancy) and W (transition) accumulation into the single backward sweep and replaced the `(N, n)` β array with two `(n,)` vectors (β[n] needs only β[n+1]) — halving the large-array memory traffic; ~3–9 % faster (bigger at low `n`). (2) **SQUAREM acceleration** (`optimize(accelerate=True)`, default): squared-extrapolation EM (Varadhan & Roland 2008 S3) with a monotonicity safeguard reaching the *identical* fixed point in far fewer maps — 2-state **81→33** maps (2.4× faster), 3-state **807→132** (6× faster) on well-separated data. (3) **Spectral cache build** (`_build_caches_eig` + `_fill_caches`): for sparse photon streams (`n ≥ 3`, `max Δt ≥ 512`) `A^Δt`/`ρ` come from an eigendecomposition via divided differences instead of binary exponentiation (1.4–2.4× faster build at `max_dt≈2200`), conditioning-guarded with a pair-power fall-back; dense data keeps pair-power. (4) Opt-in **`single_precision`** float32 hot loop + caches (~1.9× at 3 states), explicitly approximate. Also fixed a latent landmine: ChiSurf's `env_bootstrap` rewrites `NUMBA_NUM_THREADS` *after* numba's pool launches, which crashed the first late cold compile (the float32 kernel) via numba's reload guard — `optimize` now re-pins the env to numba's launched pool size (`_sync_numba_threads`). New engine tests (SQUAREM≡plain-EM + fewer maps, eig≡pair-power build, eig declines on defective `A`, float32 near float64); **21 burst_h2mm tests green** (stable across repeated full-dir runs). Net vs `H2MM_C` on the slow benchmark: **0.51×** (2-state) / **0.52×** (3-state) wall-time per map, now also converging in fewer maps. Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **Burst — H2MM A/B *performance* guard vs `H2MM_C`.** Added `test_benchmark_vs_reference` (2- and 3-state) to `tests/test_ab_vs_h2mm_c.py` as a companion to the correctness A/B tests: it times a full EM run of the numba port against the reference C `H2MM_C` on identical tttrlib-simulated data, reports **ms/EM-iteration** (JIT warmed up first, so compilation isn't charged; normalised per iteration so convergence-count differences don't skew it), and asserts (a) the two engines reach the same optimum and (b) the port stays under ~1.5× the reference time/iter — a wide-margin regression guard that would catch an `O(N·n⁴)`-style slowdown. Marked `slow` (excluded from default runs; run with `-m slow -s`). Measured on 150k-photon dense bursts: **0.68×** (2-state) / **0.50×** (3-state) the `H2MM_C` wall-time per iteration. Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **Burst — H2MM E-step now beats the reference `H2MM_C` (defer the ρ contraction).** The parallel E-step (`core/h2mm.py::_estep`) formed the transition tensor ξ *per gap* — an `O(N·n⁴)` inner loop that also streamed the whole `rho_cache` from memory once per photon — leaving the numba engine ~1.05–1.21× *slower* than `H2MM_C` (gap widening with `n_states`). Restructured to accumulate only the per-unique-Δt weight `W[slot,k,m] = Σ_gaps α[n,k]·obs[m,y_{n+1}]·β[n+1,m]/c` in the hot loop (`O(N·n²)`, thread-local), then contract `ξ[i,j] = Σ_{slot,k,m} W[slot,k,m]·ρ[slot,k,m,i,j]` **once per slot** in the serial reduction (`O(n_slots·n⁴)`, `n_slots` = unique inter-photon Δt ≪ N). Mathematically identical (ρ is fixed per slot, so the per-gap sum factors out); `rho_cache` is now read `n_slots` times instead of N. On 2000×120-photon simulated data (100 EM iters) the engine went from ~1.05×/0.95×/1.12× the `H2MM_C` wall-time at 2/3/4 states to **0.61×/0.37×/0.28×** — i.e. ~1.6×/2.7×/3.6× *faster*; the 4-state fit dropped 1253→334 ms. All 10 A/B-vs-`H2MM_C` + engine tests still green (converged logL/BIC/params and Viterbi match the reference to the same tolerances). Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **MMFDB — threaded + enforced `auth` across the whole `api.py` boundary (INC-04).** Most `api.py` functions ran unauthenticated and lacked an `auth` parameter — yet **all ~40 are registered as `mmfdb.v1.*` RPCs invoked via `h(**params, auth=auth)`**, so those versioned methods would have `TypeError`'d the moment they were called with a token: a latent break *and* the enforcement gap. Added `auth` to every one (samples/experiments/operations/parameters/graph/setups/chinet/branches/audit) with shared helpers for **progressive enforcement**: `_acting_user_id` (authenticated principal, else configured default — preserves in-process/GUI), `_new_object_acl` (writes stamp the owner + a default ACL: samples/experiments join the existing artifact/operation ACLs), `_acl_read_or_pass`/`_acl_filter_or_pass` (reads enforce ACLs *where they exist*, stay open otherwise — no legacy lockout), and `_effective_target_user` making the user-scoped branch ops **self-or-admin** (fixed an IDOR where any user could set/read another's active branch). New `tests/test_api_auth.py` (5: auth accepted + owner-ACL + cross-user filtering, graceful no-ACL reads, in-process default-user attribution, IDOR blocked, admin-may-target-other). Standalone **468 passed, 1 skipped** (was 463); `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing). Marked [INC-04](/specs/assessment.md#inc-04) 🚧 ADDRESSED. (Landed alongside a concurrent `mmfdb.admin`→`mmfdb_admin` extraction in the tree; committed only the `api.py`/test changes.)
* **Burst — new H2MM photon-by-photon HMM plugin (`burst_h2mm`).** Added a single-molecule FRET plugin that resolves sub-burst state dynamics on the microsecond scale, ported from the Pirchi *et al.* 2016 / `H2MM_C` algorithm into a **self-contained numba engine** (no external H2MM dependency). `core/h2mm.py` caches `A^Δt` and the transition-count `ρ` tensor once per **unique** inter-photon Δt via associative pair-power binary exponentiation (cost scales with photons, not clock ticks), runs a scaled forward-backward + Baum-Welch EM, and provides Viterbi+ICL, BIC, a model factory and a validation simulator. Follows the client-server standard: Qt-free `core/` (h2mm/photons/analysis) → RPC `backend/services.py` (`burst_h2mm.jobs.compute`) → thin `H2mmClient` + `H2mmTool` GUI (FRET states, transition-density plot, BIC/ICL model selection, per-state dwell-time distributions) + click CLI. Consumes `.bur` bursts sliced from `tttrlib.TTTR`, mapping routing-channel + micro-time windows to photon streams. Wired into `burst_analysis` as **step 6 "H2MM"** with workflow-context handoff (inherits burst folder + channel defs). Marked **experimental** (numba engine validated on simulated ground-truth recovery, monotonic logL, BIC selection, Viterbi, RPC, stream-gating, headless GUI). **A/B-verified numerically equivalent to the reference `H2MM_C`** on tttrlib-simulated data carried through the real `tttrlib.TTTR` + plugin extraction path: fixed-model and converged log-likelihood/BIC agree to ~1e-9, π/trans/obs to ~1e-7 (2- and 3-state), and Viterbi paths match 100% over ~36k photons (`tests/test_ab_vs_h2mm_c.py`, skips if `H2MM_C` absent). 15 tests green; not yet validated on experimental data. Documented in [burst plugins](/plugins/burst.md).
* **MMFDB Admin boundary correction — API/CLI stay in `mmfdb.admin`, GUI split to `mmfdb-admin`.** Corrected the prerelease admin ownership line: `mmfdb.admin` remains the admin API/CLI package (RPC service implementation, direct in-process API client, CLI/`mmfdb-admin` console script), while the former ChiSurf plugin GUI now lives in sibling package `modules/mmfdb-admin` as the standalone `mmfdb-admin-gui` Qt surface. The ChiSurf `core/mmfdb_admin` plugin is now a shim: manifest services point to `mmfdb.admin.backend.services`, and GUI construction delegates to `mmfdb_admin.gui.app`. Ported a ChiSurf-free AutoForm layer into `mmfdb-admin` and wired browse tabs to AutoForm-backed row detail panes. Added boundary coverage for the split and smoke-verified standalone CLI status plus offscreen GUI construction.
* **MMFDB — auth polish: external-user password guard + auth-services lint cleanup (PRD-59).** Rounding out the auth layer: `change_password` (both the token-based `auth_services.change_password_handler` and the legacy `password_services.change_password_handler`) now **rejects external-provider users** — their credential lives in the directory/IdP, so writing a local hash was meaningless (and confusing). Cleaned the pre-existing lint debt in `auth_services.py` (9 → 0), including a **real latent bug** — the `require_handler_auth` return annotation referenced undefined `sqlite3`/`Principal` (F821; harmless only because `from __future__ import annotations` stringifies it) — plus removed 5 genuinely-unused re-imports (verified nothing imports them from that module) and sorted imports; same for `password_services.py`. 1 new regression test. Standalone **462 passed, 1 skipped**; `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing). Deliberately **not** done (low value, noted): user-enumeration timing equalization on the local path — network jitter dominates PBKDF2 timing and MMFDB isn't internet-facing.
* **MMFDB — closed a critical auth hole + enforced "local always available" (PRD-59 security review).** Auditing "what else needs doing," found that an **external (LDAP) user could be logged in via the local provider with an empty password**: JIT-provisioned directory users have no local `password_hash`, and `LocalAuthProvider`'s (and legacy `password_services.login_handler`'s) "no hash → accept when no password" branch treated them as passwordless — `login(provider="local", user_id="<ldap_uid>", password="")` succeeded without the directory. Fixed by making **both** local-auth paths reject users whose `auth_provider != 'local'` (genuine local passwordless users are unaffected). Also found a **lockout risk**: with LDAP as the configured default, the bootstrap **local admin couldn't log in** (default dispatch sent everyone to LDAP). Added a **local fallback** in `login()` — when a non-local default returns `None`, the local provider is tried — making "Local always available" real; it can't weaken security because the local provider now rejects external-homed users (a directory-rejected external user still fails). Third finding: **email-match could hijack a real local account** — an LDAP user whose email collides with an existing local admin was silently relinked (`auth_provider→ldap`), handing control to whoever holds that directory account. Now `resolve_or_provision_user` only email-adopts a **placeholder** local account (local-homed, no password); a real local-credential or other-provider account is never converted (JIT mints a distinct account instead). 4 new regression tests (external-user local-login blocked incl. empty password; local admin logs in under LDAP-default; external user can't sneak through the fallback; email collision doesn't hijack a local password account). Standalone **461 passed, 1 skipped** (was 457); `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing).
* **MMFDB — auth hardening + extensible provider registry (PRD-59 Phase 4).** Made the auth layer excellent + extensible (per the "great arch, extend later" directive). **Provider registry** (`register_provider`/`build_provider`/`available_providers` + `ProviderContext` in `security/auth_providers.py`): the sole extension point — a new backend (OIDC, external-IdP, …) is a class + one registration call; `login.resolve_provider` now dispatches through it and never needs editing (built-in `local`/`ldap` self-register). **Fixed a real security bug:** failed RPC logins weren't persisted (each `login_handler` opens its own connection and the old flow raised without committing), so `is_throttled` never accumulated and brute-force throttling was silently disabled — `login._record_failure` now commits the attempt (empirically 0→≥5 persisted across connections). **Directory-authoritative reconciliation** (`sync_identity`): external-provider logins refresh `is_admin`/`email`/`display_name` and reconcile the provider's new `AuthIdentity.managed_groups` (add carried, remove absent; admin membership follows `is_admin`) while never touching locally-managed groups. **LDAP hardened:** `base_dn` validation, `try/finally` unbind, optional TLS CA (`ca_cert`→`ldap3.Tls`), and unreachable/search errors → clear `AuthError` (wrong password stays `None`). New `tests/test_auth_hardening.py` (9: registry extend/build/unknown, throttle-persists-across-RPC-connections, attribute+group reconciliation incl. de-escalation and unmanaged-group safety, LDAP missing-base-dn / directory-unavailable / managed-groups). Standalone **457 passed, 1 skipped** (was 448); `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing).
* **MMFDB — auth CLI + config surface + PRD-59 concept (PRD-59 Phase 3).** Added a headless `mmfdb-admin auth` command group (`admin/cli`): `login --user … [--password …] [--provider local|ldap]` (mints a session token via `login()`), `whoami --token …` (resolves a token to its principal), and `status` (shows the active provider + LDAP settings) — all with `--json`. Exposed a standalone `mmfdb-admin` console-script entry (`[project.scripts]`) so the path is headless-first without depending on ChiSurf's `csc` plugin mounting. New `tests/test_auth_cli.py` (4 tests via click's `CliRunner`: login→whoami round-trip, bad-password abort, invalid-token, `status` defaults local). Wrote the **PRD-59** concept (`okf/prds/prd-59.md`, status in-progress) + index/glyph and an auth-provider section in `okf/architecture/mmfdb.md`. **Correction:** Phase-1/2 log bullets and code docstrings mislabelled this work "PRD-49" (which is the unrelated Multiparameter-Fluorescence PRD); renumbered to the next free id **PRD-59** across code/tests/log (Phase-1/2 commit *messages* keep the old label — history). Standalone **448 passed, 1 skipped** (was 444); `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing). This completes the local + LDAP + CLI scope; OIDC/SAML and eLabFTW-IdP remain deferred.
* **MMFDB — LDAP authentication provider (PRD-59 Phase 2).** Added `LdapAuthProvider` to `security/auth_providers.py`: search+bind against a directory (service-account bind → search `user_filter` under `base_dn` → re-bind as the located user DN to verify the password), with `memberOf` → MMFDB group mapping (`group_map`) and admin elevation (`admin_groups`). The `ldap3` dependency is **optional + lazy** (`_require_ldap3` guard; new `[ldap]` extra in `pyproject.toml`), and the provider takes an injectable `connection_factory` so it's driven **fully offline via `ldap3` MOCK_SYNC** in tests (no live directory). LDAP-filter values are escaped (`escape_filter_chars`) against injection. Config wiring: `config.set_auth_config_resolver`/`configured_auth_config` (host resolver → `MMFDB_AUTH_PROVIDER`/`MMFDB_LDAP_*` env → `None`=local), consumed by `auth_services._auth_config`; `login.resolve_provider` already dispatches `"ldap"`. Secret handling: `security/credentials.py` gained `store/load_ldap_bind_password` (keyring, distinct service) so the bind password lives in the OS store / env, never settings JSON. New `tests/test_ldap_auth.py` (10 tests via MOCK_SYNC: attr+group mapping, wrong/empty password, unknown user, service-bind-failure → `AuthError`, filter-injection escaping, missing-dep `ImportError`, `login()` JIT-provision + group-sync end-to-end, and the env/resolver config path). Standalone **444 passed, 1 skipped** (was 434); `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing). CLI + PRD-59 concept are Phase 3.
* **MMFDB — pluggable authentication framework + Local provider (PRD-59 Phase 1).** MMFDB auth was 100% local and hardcoded (`auth_services.login_handler` → `verify_password` → `create_session`, no provider seam). Introduced a small provider layer under `security/`: `auth_providers.py` (`AuthIdentity` dataclass, `AuthProvider` `Protocol`, `LocalAuthProvider` wrapping the existing PBKDF2 `verify_password` with byte-for-byte the same admin/passwordless/no-hash rules), and `login.py` — a `login(conn, provider=…, user_id, password, …)` orchestrator that authenticates → **resolves/JIT-provisions** the MMFDB user via new `flr_sample_users.auth_provider`/`external_id` columns (match by `(auth_provider, external_id)` → `email` → JIT-create for external providers; `local` returns the row as-is) → **syncs mapped groups** into `mmfdb_group_member` → mints the existing session. `auth_services.login_handler` now delegates to it (RPC contract `{ok, authenticated, token, expires_at, user}` unchanged; the `mmfdb_admin` plugin copy is already an `import *` re-export, so nothing to reconcile). Schema: added `auth_provider TEXT DEFAULT 'local'` + `external_id TEXT` to `flr_sample_users` (non-canonical table, so the hardcoded DDL is authoritative) + index `idx_users_external(auth_provider, external_id)` for reverse lookup; **fixed the hardcoded fixed-salt bootstrap-admin password** (`_hash_admin_password`) to a per-user random salt (`admin` still verifies; two fresh DBs now differ). New `tests/test_auth_providers.py` (13 tests). PRD-59 concept to be written in Phase 3; LDAP provider is Phase 2.

Left raw (the legitimate home): the `get_photon_streams` JOIN, access-control/impact subqueries + `COUNT`s, `get_audit_logs`/`struct_ref_seq_dif` compound `ORDER BY`, and the two `*_key_value`/`optical_properties` column-projection reads. Advances [PRD-26](/prds/prd-26.md)/[INC-05](/specs/assessment.md#inc-05). Chasing the constraint-gap follow-up flagged the day before (`citeulike`/`products`/`standards`/`product_categories` "have no PK/UNIQUE"), found the real story: **those tables don't exist in the reconciled schema at all** — `SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master` returns nothing for them (the earlier `PRAGMA index_list` "PK=NONE" read was a false negative — PRAGMA on a missing table returns empty rather than erroring). The nine methods (`get_citations`/`add_citation`/`delete_citation`, `get_product_categories`/`add_product_category`, `get_products`/`add_product`, `get_standards`/`add_standard`) are **dead code** — zero callers anywhere in mmfdb/chisurf/tests, and any call would raise `no such table`. Deleted them (and their two section-comment headers) rather than leave the just-converted-but-unreachable `dao.insert` calls. The still-present `chem_descriptors`/`images`/`flr_photon_stream`/`optical_properties`/`mmfdb_vocabulary`/`flr_experiment_key_value` tables **do** exist, so those conversions stand. Standalone **421 passed, 1 skipped**. Audit: repository.py select 28 → 24, bespoke 1 → 0 (removed the citeulike `IN(...)` read). Advances [PRD-26](/prds/prd-26.md)/[INC-05](/specs/assessment.md#inc-05).

## 2026-07-07
* **MMFDB — eliminated the last `INSERT OR REPLACE` in `repository.py` + `analysis_data`; the package is now `INSERT OR REPLACE`-free (PRD-26).** With `dao.upsert` keyless-tolerant, converted the remaining 11 `INSERT OR REPLACE` writes in the repository core + the `analysis_data` one, choosing the target per table's actual constraint: **real conflict target → `dao.upsert`** — `add_vocabulary` (`field_name,value`), `set_experiment_key_value` (`experiment_id,key`), `add_optical_property` (`probe_id,property_name`), `add_photon_stream` (`stream_id`), `add_analysis_data` (`analysis_id,data_type,data_name`, keeping the load-bearing soft-delete UPDATE that dedups the NULL-`data_name` case SQLite's UNIQUE treats as distinct); **autoincrement-id / no-UNIQUE → `dao.insert`** — `add_chemical_descriptor`, `add_image`, and `add_external_file` (always-fresh uuid, so its follow-up `SELECT id` collapsed into the `insert` return). **A real finding:** `citeulike`, `products`, `standards`, `product_categories` have **neither a PK nor a UNIQUE** in the reconciled schema, so their `INSERT OR REPLACE` was silently a plain insert (duplicate ids possible) — converted to `dao.insert` with a comment making the missing-constraint gap explicit rather than pretending to upsert (a real fix needs a schema PK; noted for follow-up). Standalone **421 passed, 1 skipped**; `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing). Audit: insert 64 → 53 (repository.py 13 → 2, its only raw inserts now the append-only `mmfdb_parameter`/`mmfdb_audit_log` core). **No `INSERT OR REPLACE` remains anywhere in the package.** Advances [PRD-26](/prds/prd-26.md)/[INC-05](/specs/assessment.md#inc-05).
* **MMFDB — made `dao.upsert` keyless-tolerant; converted the last "keyless-table" `INSERT OR REPLACE` writes (PRD-26).** Root-caused the documented "keyless tables can't be upserted" limitation: `analysis_metadata` and `flr_sample_key_value` have **no PRIMARY KEY but a real `UNIQUE(…, key)`** constraint, so they *are* valid `ON CONFLICT` targets — the only blocker was `dao.upsert` calling `primary_key()` *unconditionally* at the end (to pick a return value), which raised `DaoError` before the caller's explicit `conflict` could help. Wrapped that in `try/except DaoError → return lastrowid`, mirroring the same fix already in `dao.insert`. With that, converted the three surviving `INSERT OR REPLACE` writes onto identity-preserving upserts: `SampleMixin.set_sample_key_value` (`conflict=["sample_id","key"]`) and `AnalysisMixin.add_analysis_metadata` + the `set_analysis_metadata` reseed loop (`conflict=["analysis_id","key"]`) — re-add now clears `deleted_at` in place instead of delete-and-reinsert. Added `test_upsert_into_keyless_table_on_unique` (sibling to the keyless-`insert` test). Standalone suite **421 passed, 1 skipped**. Audit: insert 67 → 64. **`flr_sample_key_value`/`analysis_metadata` are no longer a DAO limitation** — the only genuinely un-upsertable tables left are those with *neither* a PK *nor* a targetable UNIQUE. Advances [PRD-26](/prds/prd-26.md)/[INC-05](/specs/assessment.md#inc-05).
* **MMFDB — swept the trivial single-table SELECTs in `queries/*.py` onto the DAO (PRD-26).** With the scattered-CRUD migration complete, converted the *trivial* reads in the centralized query mixins — single-table select-by-PK and equality-filter reads → `db.dao.get`/`db.dao.list` — across all 13 query files (users, parameters, experiments, branches, protocols, samples, studies, analysis, setups, probes, artifacts; objects/lifecycle had none convertible). Also folded a handful of coupled raw writes that a SELECT hung off (e.g. `add_experiment_data`'s `INSERT`+`last_insert_rowid()` → `dao.insert`). **Deliberately left raw** (the legitimate centralized home): JOINs, `DISTINCT`/`COUNT`/`MAX` aggregates, `LIKE` search, `json_extract` filters, `IN (…)`/`OR` predicates, `sqlite_master`/`PRAGMA` introspection, subquery/latest-per-group, compound `ORDER BY` (the DAO's `list` is single-column), reads on the external source `spectra.db` connection, and the `consolidate_probes` merge (dynamic `probe_id`/`item_id` key columns). **Lessons that shaped the sweep:** (1) `dao.get` needs an explicit `pk_column` when the dictionary schema doesn't mark the PK and the `<table>_id`/`id`/`uuid` fallback misses (`mmfdb_protocol.protocol_id`, `mmfdb_study.study_id`, `spectra.spectrum_id` — the last has a *separate* autoincrement `id`, so the fallback would silently query the wrong column); (2) `dao.list`/`get` return plain dicts, so any caller doing positional `row[0]`/tuple-unpacking had to be checked first (only `studies.backfill` and a couple of projections kept their raw form for that reason); (3) reads with **no** `deleted_at` guard were preserved with `include_deleted=True`; (4) converting `find_or_add_probe`'s refetch to a dict incidentally fixed a latent `sqlite3.Row.get()` call. Verified after every file — standalone **420 passed, 1 skipped** throughout; `test/fio` **236 passed** (1 pre-existing `ndxplorer` fail). Audit: select **241 → 175**; `probes.py` reads reclassified into bespoke (JOIN/aggregate/source/merge), so bespoke rose 68 → 87 as an accuracy correction, not new raw SQL. This was the optional item-3 cleanup in `HANDOVER.md`; the remaining `queries/` SELECTs are all the sanctioned bespoke home. Advances [PRD-26](/prds/prd-26.md)/[INC-05](/specs/assessment.md#inc-05).
* **MMFDB — converted the last `INSERT OR REPLACE` in `queries/` (`add_operation_artifact`) to a composite-PK upsert (PRD-26).** The earlier "clear the remaining `INSERT OR REPLACE`/`IGNORE` in `queries/`" pass had left `ArtifactOpsMixin.add_operation_artifact` raw because `mmfdb_operation_artifact`'s 4-column composite PK was believed un-targetable by the DAO. Having disproven that (see the `seed_example` bullet below), routed it onto `db.dao.upsert(conflict=["operation_id","artifact_id","direction","role"])` — the `role`/`direction` are conflict columns and `created_at`/`updated_at`/`deleted_at` are the update set, so on re-add it refreshes the timestamps and **resurrects** a soft-deleted link (`deleted_at → NULL`) in place, identity-preserving where `INSERT OR REPLACE` would delete-and-reinsert. Verified the insert / soft-delete / re-add cycle keeps a single row with `deleted_at=NULL`. The two `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO mmfdb_group_member` writes in `schema.py`'s `bootstrap_*` stay `# raw` — they run on a **bare `sqlite3.Connection`** with no `dao` in scope (the documented bootstrap-seeder pattern).
